About

Design is intentional,
not accidental.

I'm Ahmad — a Visual Communication Design graduate shaped by three cultures (Syria, Saudi Arabia, Turkey) and fluent in three languages. That perspective lives in everything I create.

I don't just produce content. I think about why it exists, who it's for, and what it needs to say. Clean, direct, effective — nothing unnecessary.

Right now I'm pioneering AI-driven advertising campaigns that combine cinematic storytelling with product marketing — a space most brands haven't yet explored.

Cestals M.I Space
Visual Identity

Cestals M.I Space

Year
2025
Category
Visual Identity
Role
Brand Designer & Packaging Designer
19
Visual Identity — 2025
01

The Problem

A fun snack brand needed a full visual identity that could stand out on shelves and appeal to all ages — with a bold, memorable personality rooted in retro space adventure.

02

The Idea

I created a complete brand universe around "Cestals M.I Space Potato Chips" — a one-eyed alien mascot, retro space typography, a rich color palette of deep teal, golden yellow and crimson red, and a packaging system that wraps around a cylindrical can with three distinct sides: the UFO abduction scene, the mascot face, and the nutrition label.

03

The Result

A cohesive brand system delivered with logo, color palette, typography, packaging mockups, social media designs, brand applications (T-shirt, keychain, truck), and seamless pattern textures.

Tools Used
Adobe IllustratorPhotoshopMidjourney
Deliverables
Logo & brand guidelines
Packaging system (3-sided can)
Social media designs
Brand applications
Pattern & texture system
01

The Problem

Design premium packaging for a botanical sparkling soda brand — Chola — with three flavours: Peony & Rhubarb, Rose & Raspberry, and Orange Blossom & White Tea.

02

The Idea

Each flavour gets its own tonal gradient — deep burgundy, blush pink, and warm gold — with botanical floral illustration and refined serif typography. The result is elegant, premium, and shelf-ready.

03

The Result

A cohesive three-variant packaging system that communicates botanical sophistication. The design positions Chola as a premium lifestyle product.
